Title: Summa doctrinae christianae, per quaestiones conscripta (Canisius)
Creator: Peter Canisius (Petrus Canisius), Society of Jesus
Publisher: apud Seth Viottum (1565; publisher line as the archive record gives it)
Publication Date: 1565
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: Printed in 1565 (title page: apud Seth Viottum); public domain by the project's date
  rule (published 1930 or earlier). On Internet Archive, the item metadata carries no rights field;
  that tag is supporting evidence only. The scan is a Google Books digitisation hosted on Internet
  Archive; the usage request in its front matter does not claim rights in the public-domain work.
  Checked 2026-09-30.
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_ow9qll94C8MC (archive.org/details/bub_gb_ow9qll94C8MC),
  full-text OCR file bub_gb_ow9qll94C8MC_djvu.txt, fetched directly (curl, raw bytes) 2026-09-30 by
  the Library thread; the body below is that file unaltered.
Provenance note: Body unaltered, including its own OCR errors. Header prepended by the Library
  thread. A Google Books scan hosted on Internet Archive; the printing is a sixteenth-century Latin
  edition of the catechism, not a modern edition. The front matter carries Google's usage request,
  which does not claim rights in the public-domain work.
Content note: Canisius's Latin catechism (the Summa doctrinae christianae) with the marginal
  scripture and patristic citations printed in the outer margins.
